機器人和半導體設備需求強勁，惟評價合理 
 1Q26 毛利率受惠產能利 用率上揚和產品結構優化，優於預期，稅後
EPS 1.64 元：上銀 1Q26 營收為 63.7 億元 ，-1.9%QoQ，+9.3%Y oY；
1Q26 區域別比重為亞洲 56%、歐洲 28%、美洲 8%、台灣 8%。產品
組合為滾珠螺桿 20%、線性滑軌 60%、機器人 12%、其他 9%。獲利
方面，毛利率 31.9%，優於預期之 29.3%，係因 1)人民幣升值影響約
1.2 個百分點、2)高毛利的歐洲佔比增加、3)產能利用率上揚，營益率
9.9%，優於預期之 9.1%，業外部份，匯兌利益挹注約 1.03 億元，稅
後 EPS 1.64 元，優於預期之 1.06 元。 
 產業谷底復甦，調整 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 8.49 元：3M26 日本工具機
訂單達 1,935 億日圓，+28.1%Y oY，累積+26%Y oY，美國市場受惠半
導體與航太需求帶動累計+48.3%Y oY，中國市場受惠汽車與自動化需
求，累計+46.2%Y oY，歐洲市場也重回成長軌道，累計+19.9%Y oY，
2026 年工具機市場已進入景氣回升階段。展望後市，公司對 2026 年
各地區看法 : 美國回升力道不錯，美國製造效應發酵，台灣工具機銷
往美國有很多機會 ， 歐洲汽車動能恢復仍不佳 ， 自動化有工業4.0 的持
續推升表現相對穩定，中國市場則因機器人、半導體、 AI 相關需求很
熱，來自中國經銷反饋，認為工具機市場到年底都很熱；機器人部門
為 2026 年成長主軸 ， 主要以1)半導體設備需求隨半導體客戶設廠挹注
營運動能；2)美系物流機器人：1H26 客戶小量投入生產線，初步金額
貢獻有限，最快 2H26 會增加出貨，總結前述，調整 2026 年營收至
285.8 億元(原 259.2 億元)，+8.5%Y oY。獲利部分，受惠訂單回籠、
產能利用率將逐步回升，加計下半年漲價效應，價量齊揚下有望推升
獲利逐季走揚，故調整 2026 年稅 EPS 至 8.49 元(原 5.58 元)。 
 營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維持中立評等，目標價 350 元：評價部
分，當前公司評價處 42x，評價偏高，故維持中立評等，目標價 350
元(41x2026 EPS)。

上銀科技為全球第二大線性滑軌
廠，滾珠螺桿市佔率全球第一 
 上銀科技為全球第二大線性滑軌廠 
上銀科技股份有限公司為全球第二大線性滑軌廠，總部位於台灣台中工業區，
主要從事精密滾珠螺桿、線性傳動元件與工業型機械人研發製造，以自有品牌
「HIWIN」行銷全球。2025 年產品別：線性滑軌 60%、滾珠螺桿 20%、機器
人 11%與其他 9%；區域別：亞洲 56%、歐洲 28%、美洲 8%、台灣 8%。

1Q26 毛利率受惠產能利 用率上
揚和產品結構優化，稅後 EPS 
1.64 元

1Q26 毛利率受惠產能利用率上揚和產品結構優化，稅後 EPS 1.64 元 
上銀 1Q26 營收為 63.7 億元，-1.9%QoQ，+9.3%Y oY；1Q26 區域別比重為
亞洲 56%、歐洲 28%、美洲 8%、台灣 8%。產品組合為滾珠螺桿 20%、線性
滑軌 60%、機器人 12%、其他 9%。獲利方面，毛利率 31.9%，優於預期之
29.3%，係因 1)人民幣升值影響約 1.2 個百分點、2)高毛利的歐洲佔比增加、
3)產能利用率上揚，營益率 9.9%，優於預期之 9.1%，業外部份，匯兌利益挹
注約 1.03 億元，稅後 EPS 1.64 元，優於預期之 1.06 元。

2Q26 營運展望正向 ， 訂單能見度
上看 4.5-5 個月，預估稅後 EPS 
2.11 元

2Q26 營運展望正向，訂單能見度上看 4.5-5 個月，預估稅後 EPS 2.11 元 
上銀 4 月營收為 24.7 億元，+5.0%MoM，+20.7%Y oY，營收達成率 34%，
優於預期，係因線軌與滾珠螺桿兩大產品線溫和復甦。展望後市，目前訂單能
見度滾珠螺桿 4.5-5 個月 、 線性滑軌3.5 個月 ， 農歷年至今都在加班去化訂單 ，
公司表示接下來幾個月的營收獲利不會低於 3-4 月 。 此外 ， 管理層表示反應原
物料上漲 ，3 月有調漲售價 ， 不同產品 、 不同區域漲幅不同 ， 平均漲幅10-15%。
考量產品交期長達 3-4 個月， 預計漲價效益在2Q26 季底才會顯現， 故元富調
整 2Q26 營收至 71.7 億元(原 61.3 億元)，+12.4%QoQ，+21%Y oY。獲利部
分，在產能利用率上升，且費用支出較為固定下，對毛利看法正向，不考慮一
次性業外損益下，調整稅後 EPS 至 2.11 元(原 0.96 元)。

產業谷底復甦 ， 調整2026 年稅後
EPS 至 8.49 元 
 產業谷底復甦，調整 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 8.49 元 
3M26 日本工具機訂單達 1,935 億日圓，+31.9%MoM，+28.1%Y oY，累積
+26%Y oY，美國市場受惠半導體與航太需求帶動累計+48.3%Y oY，中國市場
受惠於汽車與自動化需求，累計 +46.2%Y oY，歐洲市場也重回成長軌道，累
計+19.9%Y oY，2026 年工具機市場已進入景氣回升階段。展望後市，公司對
2026 年各地區看法 : 美國回升力道不錯，川普美國製造效應發酵，台灣工具
機銷往美國有很多機會，歐洲汽車動能回復仍不佳，自動化需求有工業 4.0 的
持續推升表現相對穩定，中國市場則因機器人、半導體、 AI 相關需求很熱，
來自中國經銷反饋，認為中國工具機市場到年底都很熱；機器人部門為 2026
年成長主軸 ， 主要以1)半導體設備(晶圓機器人何晶圓移載系統)需求隨半導體
客戶設廠挹注營運動能；2)美系物流機器人：1H26 客戶小量投入生產線，初
步金額貢獻有限，最快 2H26 會逐步增加出貨，總結前述，調整 2026 年營收
至 285.8 億元(原 259.2 億元)，+8.5%Y oY。獲利部分，受惠訂單回籠、產能
利用率將逐步回升，加計下半 年漲價效應，價量齊揚下有望推升獲利逐季走
揚，故調整 2026 年稅 EPS 至 8.49 元(原 5.58 元)。

營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維
持中立評等，目標價 350 元 
 營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維持中立評等，目標價 350 元 
管理層對 2026 年營運展望正向，且 AI 資料中心的興起帶動技術革新，新製程
採用帶動眾多電子行業的擴廠動作、設備採購，設備零件、次系統或自動化配
套需求明顯增加。過往評價區間處 18-40x 水準，當前公司評價處 42x，評價偏
高，故維持中立評等，目標價 350 元(41x2026 EPS)。

公司研究報告

條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。

2382 廣達電腦(Quanta)

報告日期 ：2026/05/21           研究員 ： 曾智楷(Kevin Tseng) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 AI 伺服器比重提高及一次性費用影響， 1Q26 毛利
率降至 4.78% 
廣達 1Q26 營收 8092 億， QoQ +26.7% ，YoY +66.6% ，受惠於
伺服器與筆記型電腦出貨強勁，帶動 1Q26 營收成長。而產品組
合部分， 1Q26 公司伺服器營收已超過整體營收比例 8 成， AI 伺
服器營收季增超過三成，佔整體伺服器營收比重超過 75% ，通用
型伺服器第一季表現亦優於傳統淡季，呈現雙位數季增。此外，
1Q26 NB 出貨 1000 萬台， QoQ -8.3% ，YoY -7.4% ，公司表示，
受惠於主要客戶新機種上市積極拉貨，整體 NB 出貨呈現淡季不
淡，再加上高單價產品比重提升、關鍵零組件漲價，第一季筆電
出貨單價較上季激增超過 30%。 而獲利方面 ，1Q26 營業毛利 386.9
億， QoQ -4.2% 。YoY +0.6% ，毛利率 4.78% ，QoQ -1.55ppts ，
YoY -3.14ppts ，公司表示，約三分之二影響來自產品組合變化，
高單價 AI 機櫃出貨成長，以至於毛利率稀釋，另外三分之一則由
於筆電產品組合以及認列一次性 AI 伺服器產品費用影響。 1Q26
營業利益 230.2 億， QoQ -4.1% ，YoY -6.4% ，營利率 2.85% ，
QoQ -0.91ppts ，YoY -2.22ppts ， 業外淨利 15.1 億 ， 稅後淨利211.9
億， QoQ -4.5% ，YoY +8.7% ，單季稅後 EPS 5.50 元。

二、 2Q26 營利率可望改善，預估 EPS 6.14 元 
公司公告 4 月營收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累計營收 1.15 兆， YoY +79.6% 。展望 2Q26 ，公司預期 AI 伺服
器營收將維持雙位數的季成長，通用伺服器亦將呈現季增。而筆
電部分，由於第一季的基期較高，再加上 CPU 及記憶體供貨持續
緊俏，預估第二季筆電出貨量將呈現持平到小幅季增。此外，第
一季產生的一次性 AI 伺服器市場費用，第二季將不會再發生，且
第二季營業費用率預計將持續控制在 2%以下 ， 帶動第二季營業利
潤率獲得改善。預估 2Q26 營收 9083 億， QoQ +12.2% ，YoY 
+80.2% ，毛利率 5.2% ，營利率 3.4% ，稅後淨利 237 億元，單季
稅後 EPS 6.14 元。

三、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 24.92 元 
展望 2026 年，公司預期 2026 年 AI 伺服器營收將維持三位數成
長，今年下半年 ASIC 伺服器出貨量預期將呈現倍數成長，
non-NVIDIA GPU 相關專案下半年預期也將有高雙位數的成長，
投資評等：買進 
目標價格：375 元

### Page 2

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
2 
對公司整體獲利結構的改善皆有正面助益。而針對 AI 伺服器對毛
利率稀釋的影響，公司表示，目前持續與 CSP 客戶協商，預計下
半年將有少部分專案轉為 Consignment 模式 (代買代料 ， 不認列關
鍵零組件營收 )，預期將能改善毛利率結構並減輕營運資金壓力。
公司亦強調，即使扣除下半年導入 Consignment 模式的影響，全
年 AI 伺服器營收依然能維持三位數成長。而其他產品方面，公司
將今年全年通用伺服器展望自原先預估的持平，上修至雙位數年
成長；筆電部分，受到記憶體及 CPU 等關鍵零組件漲價影響，客
戶對下半年的規劃轉趨保守，公司預期下半年出貨量將無法維持
上半年的成長率，對全年筆電出貨持保守看法；車載部分，公司
預估今年車載業務營收佔比仍為個位數，儘管公司持續贏得新專
案，但由於車載產品生命週期較長，預期新專案出貨顯著貢獻將
落在 2027 年之後。資本支出方面， 2026 年資本支出預估將達到
新台幣 300 億元，主要投入於美國與泰國廠區的 AI 產能擴充，維
持今年底前 AI 產能規模較去年倍增的目標。預估公司 2026 全年
營收 3.74 兆， YoY +76% ，毛利率 5.0% ，營利率 3.1% ，稅後淨
利 962.7 億， YoY +28.4% ，全年稅後 EPS 24.92 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 375 元 
受惠於 AI 伺服器的強勁需求，且美系四大 CSP 資本支出展望維
持正向，公司維持今年 AI 伺服器營收三位數成長展望，且隨下半
年 GPU 、ASIC 伺服器專案開始放量，將有望挹注公司營運持續
增長，對公司長期展望維持正向。此外，公司公告配發現金股利
15.60 元，以公司目前股價計算，現金殖利率約為 5.38% 。以公司
2026 年 EPS 來看，目前股價交易於 11~12 倍本益比，評價仍具
上修空間 ， 給予買進投資建議 ， 目標價為 375 元(15x 2026 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
廣達電腦 (Quanta) 為全球筆記型電腦專業研發設計製造與雲端運
算解決方案領導供應商，為全球前五大筆記型電腦代工廠商。公
司主要替品牌業者從事代工服務，代工項目包含筆記型電腦、平
板電腦、智慧型手機、伺服器、商用及家用智慧物聯網裝置、消
費性電子產品、車用電子等。公司總部位於桃園龜山，生產基地
包含桃園、大陸上海、重慶、江蘇、美國田納西州、加州、墨西
哥、德國、泰國等。公司 2025 年伺服器業務 (包含 AI 伺服器 )佔
整體營收比重近 80% ，筆電 (PC) 及其他業務佔約 20% 。2025 年
銷售地區比重：美國 73% 、大陸 5%、荷蘭 2%、日本 3%、其他
17% 。筆記型電腦主要客戶為 Apple 、HP、Dell 、華碩、宏碁，

二. 營運概況及展望  
圖一、廣達製造基地及全球據點

資料來源：廣達、宏遠投顧彙整

1. 全球九大 CSP 今年資本支出估計達 8,300 億美元  
為因應全球強勁的 AI 發展需求，包含美系的微軟、 Google 、亞馬
遜(AWS) 、Meta 、甲骨文，以及中系的阿里巴巴、字節跳動、騰
訊 、 百度等全球九大雲端服務業者 (CSP) 紛紛上修今年度的資本支
出。根據 TrendForce 預估，全球九大 CSP 今年的合計資本支出
將大幅拉高至 8,300 億美元左右，年增率攀升至 79% ；其中北美
業者的成長幅度最為強勁， AWS 更以超過 2,300 億美元，相較去
年成長逾五成，持續穩居一線  CSP 資本支出規模之冠，而中系
業者則以阿里巴巴與字節跳動為海外擴張主力。 TrendForce 表
示，這波以 GPU 叢集建置及自研 ASIC 晶片為核心的基礎建設投
資，不僅驅動全球資料中心數量與電力總容量快速攀升，更預期
將讓今年全球 AI 伺服器的總用電量正式超越一般通用伺服器，進
而帶動液冷散熱與 HVDC 等關鍵零組件市場的強勁成長。

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 28% 
根據 TrendForce 預 估，全 球AI 伺服器出貨量有望維持強勁增長，
年增率預估將突破 28% ，並同步帶動整體伺服器市場出貨量年增
12.8% 。受惠於北美五大 CSP 業者 (Google 、 AWS 、 Meta 、
Microsoft 、Oracle)2026 年資本支出總額預計大幅年增 40% ，為
AI 基礎設施提供了堅實的資金支撐。其次，需求重心由「訓練」
轉向 「推論」，隨 著 AI Agents 、LLaMA 模型應用及 Copilot 升級，
雲端大廠為因應龐大的推論流量與變現需求，不僅持續追加 AI 伺
服器訂單，更同步帶動通用型伺服器 (General Server) 進入新一輪
的汰換與擴張週期。最後，自研 ASIC 的擴張動能，在追求成本優
化與特定任務效率的驅動下， 2026 年 ASIC AI 伺服器出貨占比預
計將攀升至 27.8% ，其出貨增速將超越 GPU 機種；儘管 GPU 仍
保有近七成市佔，且以  NVIDIA GB300 系列為市場主流、 VR200 
預計於下半年逐步放量，但 ASIC 出貨比重的顯著提升，已成為
2026 年伺服器市場結構性擴張的關鍵指標。

3. CSP 加速自研 ASIC 布局，取代 GPU 成長動能  
儘管目前以 NVIDIA 為首的 GPU 仍是 AI 訓練的主流 ， 且四大CSP
資本支出維持高檔 ， 但隨著 AI 模型規模的快速擴張 ， GPU 價格高
昂和供應緊張的問題日益突出，以至於大型 CSP 轉向投入自研晶
片 ASIC，CSP 透過自研 ASIC 晶片，目的在於針對自家應用進行
最佳化設計，以顯著提升效能功耗比，藉此降低成本並提供差異
化服務，同時，確保供應鏈的自主性與競爭優勢。

從四大 CSP 的自研 ASIC 出貨態勢看來，隨著 AWS Trainium 3 、
Google TPU v7 、Meta MTIA 系列等產品陸續量產，預期 2026
年 ASIC 需求將維持強勁增長動能，根據 TrendForce 預估，以
AI 伺服器總量的成長率來看，雲端服務商 (CSP) 自研的 ASIC 晶
片， 2026 年成長率會來到 44.6% ，大幅超越 GPU 的 16.1% 。

圖四、 GPU/ASIC 伺服器成長率預估

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

4. 提前拉貨需求， 1Q26 全球 PC 出貨微增 2.5%  
根據國際數據資訊 (IDC) 發布的初步報告，儘管面臨記憶體供應短
缺與總體經濟疲軟等挑戰， 2026 年第一季全球個人電腦 (PC) 出貨
量仍達到 6,560 萬台，較去年同期成長 2.5% 。這次的成長動能主
要受惠於預期零組件價格上漲的提前購買效應 、 Windows 10 的換
機潮，以及各 PC 品牌推出的新產品。然而，中東衝突導致的物
流受阻與能源價格飆升影響，再加上零組件價格持續上漲，已讓
各地區的成長出現放緩跡象 。 IDC 預 期，今 年 後 續PC 出貨量將可
能面臨進一步下滑的壓力。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一、 2Q26 營利率可望改善，預估 EPS 6.14 元 
公司公告 4 月營收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累計營收 1.15 兆， YoY +79.6% 。展望 2Q26 ，公司預期 AI 伺服
器營收將維持雙位數的季成長，通用伺服器亦將呈現季增。而筆
電部分，由於第一季的基期較高，再加上 CPU 及記憶體供貨持續
緊俏，預估第二季筆電出貨量將呈現持平到小幅季增。此外，第
一季產生的一次性 AI 伺服器市場費用，第二季將不會再發生，且
第二季營業費用率預計將持續控制在 2%以下 ， 帶動第二季營業利
潤率獲得改善。預估 2Q26 營收 9083 億， QoQ +12.2% ，YoY 
+80.2% ，毛利率 5.2% ，營利率 3.4% ，稅後淨利 237 億元，單季
稅後 EPS 6.14 元。

二、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 24.92 元 
展望 2026 年，公司預期 2026 年 AI 伺服器營收將維持三位數成
長，今年下半年 ASIC 伺服器出貨量預期將呈現倍數成長，
non-NVIDIA GPU 相關專案下半年也預期會有高雙位數的成長，
對公司整體獲利結構的改善皆有正面助益。而針對 AI 伺服器對毛
利率稀釋的影響，公司表示，目前持續與 CSP 客戶協商，預計下
半年將有少部分專案轉為 Consignment 模式 (代買代料 ， 不認列關
鍵零組件營收 )，預期將能改善毛利率結構並減輕營運資金壓力。
公司亦強調，即使扣除下半年導入 Consignment 模式的影響，全
年 AI 伺服器營收依然能維持三位數成長。其他產品方面，公司將

估今年車載業務營收佔比仍為單位數，儘管公司持續贏得新專
案，但由於車載產品生命週期較長，預期新專案出貨顯著貢獻將
落在 2027 年之後。資本支出方面， 2026 年資本支出預估將達到
新台幣 300 億元，主要投入於美國與泰國廠區的 AI 產能擴充，維
持今年底前 AI 產能規模較去年倍增的目標。預估公司 2026 全年

No turning back
Now at the centre of Google’s TPU strategy
Recent TPU roadmap changes suggest Google is no longer treating MediaTek as
merely an alternative supplier. Instead, it appears to be increasingly positioned at

the centre of Google’s next-generation TPU strategy, while Broadcom’s role
becomes more complementary. We believe the market is not yet fully reflecting
this strategic shift, and after raising our 2028 EPS forecast by 38%, we are now
68% above consensus. We lift our TP from NT$4,500 to NT$5,922 and reiterate
High Conviction Outperform.
Critical strategy changes in TPU v9 supply chains
Google recently cancelled Pumafish (Broadcom's original TPU v9) after the project

had kicked off only around a month, and replaced it with a new one, Whalefish.
Whalefish, is more like a version of ‘Sunfish Ultra’, which puts two Sunfish (TPU 8i)
together. This implies Broadcom's v9 is likely to stay at 3nm with HBM3e, while
MediaTek's v9 (Humufish, aka A5922) will migrate to 2nm with HBM4e.
No longer merely an alternative solution for Google
We believe Google’s latest TPU strategy marks a meaningful shift in positioning
between MediaTek and Broadcom. While MediaTek’s project was previously viewed

by the market as a secondary program with uncertain volume, we now see it
increasingly becoming the core platform within Google’s roadmap, with Broadcom’s
solution taking on a more backup or less strategic role. Reflecting this shift and our
supply chain checks, we raise our 2028 Humufish shipment forecast to 2.4m units
(from 1.6m) and increase our previously conservative ASP assumption to US$15k,
leading to US$36bn revenue contribution, or 63% of total sales in 2028SP.

Potential inflection points in smartphone
Our checks suggest MediaTek is working on supplying modified D9500 for
Samsung’s Galaxy S27 FE. However, the project is still being verified by Samsung
with fairly demanding requirements, and final market share will depend on the
results. We currently do not factor in any Samsung contribution but see potential
upside if the project enters mass production.
Earnings revisions and valuations
We raise our 2028 EPS forecast by 38% to reflect the higher TPU contribution,

bringing our TP to a Street high of NT$5,922 (up from NT$4,500), still based on 30x
2H27-1H28SP PE. Reiterate High Conviction Outperform.

Source: SinoPac Source: SinoPac
Investment thesis
We are seeing greater visibility into MediaTek's TPU business, with Google asking it
to pull forward the schedule. Lukewarm smartphone demand and sustained margin
pressure are likely to remain key near-term drags, and we believe the market could
react negatively if China’s weekly sell-out data shows a disappointing trend at the

beginning of 2026, which is likely to create an entry point. However, we expect
investor focus to increasingly shift towards the company’s long-term opportunity
in AI semiconductors, and we view the current valuation as undemanding compared
with ASIC peers (trading at 30-55x).
Catalysts
1) Higher-than-expected GPM, 2) market share gain in the high-end smartphone
segment, 3) smoother verification process for TPUs.
Valuation details
We use PE as our valuation methodology as IC design fabless companies typically

deliver higher growth, and we believe the market will focus primarily on its earnings
outlook. We have previously used a historical PE range to value MediaTek based on
our evaluation of the company's product cycle at the time. Our valuation is about
+2sd of its five-year average to reflect robust EPS growth driven by better smart
edge business and higher TPU forecasts.
Investment risks
1) Worse-than-expected smartphone demand globally; 2) a greater-than-expected

impact from share loss in the smartphone SoC market; 3) higher wafer prices.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Ta Liang Technology is a PCB routing and drilling machine 
maker serving PCB manufacturers, with high-end Charge-Coupled Devices 
(CCDs) commanding more than 2x the ASP of regular machines. The 
company operates production facilities in Taoyuan, Lianshui, and Nanjing, 
with a combined monthly capacity of c.300 units. CCDs are primarily 
adopted for back-drilling, though through-hole precision demand is 
increasingly emerging as an incremental growth driver. Ta Liang is also 
launching a patent-protected PCB inner layer thickness metrology (TM) 
machine to complement CCDs at an ideal TM:CCD ratio of 1:8, while 
additionally serving the semiconductor industry with metrology, AOI, and 
automation solutions.

Highlights: Ta Liang and Schmoll (not listed, Germany) are the only 
machine makers globally owning in-house controllers, creating a strong 
technology moat in the CCD market. Ta Liang continues to gain share from 
Schmoll through competitive pricing, solid brand recognition among PCB 
makers, and favourable domestic policy tailwinds supporting China-made 
equipment. In standard drilling and routing, Chinese competitors Vega CNC 
(not listed) and Hans Laser (002008 CH, NR) hold significantly larger 
capacity, though the competitive landscape in this segment has remained 
relatively stable. The patent-protected TM machine, which bundles 
naturally with CCDs, provides an additional lever to deepen customer 
relationships and reinforce share gains in the high-end segment.

Looking ahead, we see multiple growth catalysts supporting Ta Liang's 
growth trajectory. Demand for CCDs is expanding beyond back-drilling into 
through-hole processes where precision requirements are increasing, while 
the TM machine serves as a natural bundle driver to further accelerate 
CCD adoption and deepen wallet share per customer. CCDs carry high 
ASPs and deliver more than double the margins of regular machines, 
driving a favourable product mix shift alongside steady growth in regular 
machine demand underpinned by PCB makers’ expanding capex cycle. 
Reflecting strong commercial momentum, Ta Liang has secured orders at 
full capacity through 2026, with near-term upside from outsourced capacity 
additions of c.50 incremental units for regular machines and a potential 
expansion from the current single working shift to a maximum of three 
shifts in Nan Jing Plant.

Valuation: The stock is trading at 108x PER and 23x PBR for 2025 (vs. its 
PBR range of 1-10x prior to 2026), reflecting the market’s recognition of Ta 
Liang’s high-end product transition and earnings upside potential from 
CCDs and TM machine.

25 May 2026

High-precision moat in PCB machines

➢ Sole Chinese PCB machine maker with an in-house CCD controller 
➢ Capacity fully booked through 2026 
➢ TM machine bundles drive deeper wallet share from customers

Document 062: 260525_ms_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/260525_ms_MTK.pdf

### Page 1

M
Idea
MediaTek is working closely with Intel to enable the 2nm TPU “Humufish": On its
latest earnings call, MediaTek indicated for the first time that it expects 2nm ASIC to
enter mass production by 2027, but investors remained concerned about production
volumes in 2028, as Intel's EMIB-T was not proven in a large-scale ASIC project. In
our prior report
, we assumed a conservative 2028 volume of 1mn units of  2nm TPU,
implying ~US$20bn of total AI ASIC revenue. However, our recent industry checks

suggest upside for next
‑gen 2nm TPU, "Humufish", given (1) more ABF substrate
capacity, as we believe major EMIB substrate suppliers currently include Ibiden,
Unimicron, Shinko, and AT&S, and SEMCO could be added;  (2) EMIB yield at Intel
foundry is already high (>90%), ie yield improvement from embedding technology/
TGV (Through Glass Via); and  (3) the rest of the supply chain is in place, including
bumping (PTI, etc.), silicon capacitor (
AP Memory/Semco), and silicon bridge die

(from Intel's foundry). We now expect at least 2.5mn units of  2nm TPU in 2028,
mainly based on Intel EMIB, implying at least US$37bn of total TPU revenue (2.5mn
units of 2nm TPU at US$13k ASP, and 1mn units of 3nm TPU at $4.5k ASP).
Beyond 2nm TPU, MediaTek engages in the 1.4nm TPU v10 "Icefish", supporting
stronger growth from 2029 onward: Our latest supply chain checks suggest
MediaTek is likely to secure another TPU project (potentially v10) beyond the

current 2nm TPU. Although the customer may adopt a COT (customer-owned
tooling) model with potential dual-foundry supply (TSMC and Samsung foundry),
we believe MediaTek could still enjoy strong revenue/earnings growth from 2029.
We expect MediaTek’s gross margin for the Google TPU to decline from c.40% in
2026, to around 30%-35% in 2029, but operating margin may stay at 25%-30%,
accretive to corporate operating margin at around 20%-25%. We therefore raise our

intermediate growth rate for MediaTek from 9% to 12%. If MediaTek also started to
provide a full-rack turnkey service in 2028, there could be more revenue upside.
Raise PT to NT$5,088: We estimate Google TPU to account for 38% and 63% of
MediaTek’s revenue in 2027 and 2028, making the stock one of the purest Google
TPU plays in Asia Tech. Our new PT of NT$5,088 implies 38x our 2027e EPS and 18x
our 2028e EPS. We believe the stock deserves a re-rating, given a strong TPU

pipeline with close to 50% revenue CAGR over 2025–28e. We believe the agentic AI
replacement cycle may also start in 2027 for high-end Android phones, driven by
Gemini Spark
– another option value on MediaTek.

### Page 2

Idea
2
Strong AI ASIC upside in 2028 from 2nm TPU
The 2nm TPU will adopt dual sourcing – both TSMC CoWoS‑L and Intel EMIB
TSMC’s CoWoS (Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate) is the industry’s leading 2.5D packaging
solution, particularly for AI and HPC applications. Its key advantage lies in the use of a
high-density interposer or RDL layer, which enables extremely wide, low-latency
connections between logic dies and HBM stacks. This makes it well suited for bandwidth-

intensive workloads such as GPUs and AI accelerators.
However, these benefits come at a cost: CoWoS – particularly the silicon interposer/bridge
– is expensive, capacity-constrained, and subject to yield challenges, including potential
warpage issues at larger chip sizes. Cost and manufacturing complexity remain key trade-
offs. As a result, we see TSMC’s CoWoS roadmap supporting only up to ~9.7x reticle size,
which does not appear sufficient for future AI or HPC chip designs.

We expect MediaTek to secure a portion of CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure
minimum volume production, but we believe it will use Intel’s (covered by Joseph Moore)
EMIB-T as the primary solution to reduce costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution
proceeds well.
Exhibit 1: TSMC's roadmap for interposer size
Source: Winway, TSMC
Exhibit 2: Intel's EMIB can easily support larger chips  with
more reticles  (>12x) if its supply chain execution proceeds well

Source: Intel
Margin trend and full‑rack system support. In response to our question on providing
full-rack support during its earnings call (referencing recent Broadcom-Google
collaboration), MediaTek indicated it is in discussions with the customer. Management
expects any full‑rack system design support to materialize after 2027.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value addition to the customer
across both silicon and packaging design. As a result, ASIC should remain accretive to

Morgan Stanley Research 3
operating margins

Google 2nm TPU the major growth driver for MediaTek from 
2028 
On April 22, Google introduced TPU v8 at Google Cloud Next, featuring two purpose-built 
architectures for training and inference: TPU 8t and TPU 8i. Our recent supply chain 
checks across multiple companies on reticle size confirm that MediaTek’s 3nm TPU 8 
corresponds to TPU 8t. This supports our view that MediaTek’s TPU performance should 
be sufficient for AI training.
According to Google’s announcement, “both chips will be generally available later this 
year and can be used as part of Google’s AI Hypercompute,” in line with MediaTek’s 
guidance that AI ASIC will contribute ~US$2bn of revenue in 2026 (we now assume 450-
500k units at a US$4,500 ASP).
In addition, our recent checks suggest that ASP for the next-generation 2nm Humufish 
TPU, supported by MediaTek, could exceed our prior expectations, driven by an increased 
number of main dies. For Humufish, checks across multiple sources indicate the number of 
main dies per chip will significantly exceed Zebrafish (one compute die with one I/O die). 
Combined with a SerDes upgrade and larger packaging size, we estimate ASP at US$12–
15k (we now model US$13k).
At the gross margin level, management indicated margins vary by project, which may also 
reflect potential full-rack system support. We model 2nm TPU gross margin at c.35% (vs. 
c.40% for 3nm TPU) given larger revenue scale, while we still expect operating margin at 
~20–25%, above the smartphone BU’s 15–20%.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Android AI agent phones as an emerging trend from Google
I/O
Android AI agent phones – a trend to watch: Google is actively rolling out AI agents on
Android phones, starting with Galaxy S26 and Pixel 10 in early 2026. These agents can
perform multi-step tasks across apps – for example, ordering food, organizing notes, or
creating calendar events through direct interaction with app UIs. Over time, we expect
broader adoption across the Android ecosystem. Token consumption and cost remain key

constraints for agentic AI, and we believe on-device computing on edge devices such as
smartphones will help alleviate part of this cost burden.
Google I/O 2026 – Gemini evolves into an Agentic platform: At Google I/O 2026,
Google repositioned Gemini from a chatbot to a proactive AI “personal agent” capable of
autonomously handling tasks across apps and services. Google introduced several new
technologies, including Gemini Spark – a persistent assistant operating in the background

across Workspace tools – Gemini 3.5 Flash for faster real-time interaction, and Gemini
Omni for advanced multimodal capabilities spanning text, audio, images, and video. The
company also announced AI-driven changes to Google Search and deeper Gemini
integration across Android, YouTube, shopping, and future XR devices, signaling a broader
push to embed AI as a continuously available assistant that manages workflows and
automates user tasks. We believe the agentic AI replacement cycle could start in 2027

from high-end Android phones – another option value on MediaTek.
Exhibit 8: Gemini transforms into 'agentic' platform
Source: Google

Raising price target from NT$2,988 to NT$5,088
We continue to derive our price target (our base case scenario value) from a residual
income model. The price target change reflects our EPS estimate revisions, which are
higher in outer years.  In addition to raising the intermediate growth rate to 12% (from 9%)
given the strong TPU pipeline, our other key valuation assumptions remain unchanged:
cost of equity at 9.2% (beta of 1.2, risk-free rate of 2%, and risk premium of 6%) and

terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull and bear case scenario values also rise from  NT$4,100 and  NT$1,520  to NT
$6,988 and  NT$2,588, respectively.

8
Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026; we think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is not.
We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average, supported
by strong TPU demand. Our new price target of NT$5,088 implies 38x 2027e EPS.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

GPTC hosted its 2Q26 analyst meeting on May 26, 2026.
Management provided updates on 2026-2028 demand and
capacity outlook, growth drivers across next-generation advanced

packaging, and GM dynamics. Most notably, management for the
firs
t time laid out a multi-year demand and capacity framework
extending through 2028, with demand guided to grow at a ~45%
CAGR and capacity at a ~50% CAGR, reinforcing our view that
GPTC remains in an extended structural upcycle well beyond 2027.
On the back of stronger demand visibility and higher GM uplift from
3D packaging mix shift, we revise our 2026E-28E estimates by
1.2%/-7.4%/23% and raise our 12m TP to NT$4,500 (from

NT$4,000) based on 35x of 2H27-1H28E EPS (from 2027E
previously). With 45% implied upside, we reiterate our Buy rating on
GPTC.
New capacity & demand guidance points to multi-year tightness
Management laid out its first explicit multi-year demand and
capacity outlook through 2028 at this meeting, with demand guided
to grow at a 45% CAGR and capacity expansion at a slightly faster
~50% CAGR, reaffirming GPT C’s previous capacity expansion plan.
With GPTC’s Phase 2 fab having now entered production, annual

capacity is on track to reach ~200 units in 2026, with another c.100
units of capacity scheduled to come online by end-2027 and total
annual output stepping up to ~450 units by 2028. The multi-year
ramp is also supported by a significant hea dcount expansion
(already up to 350 employees as of May 2026 vs. 250 in end of
2025) alongside an ongoing transition toward higher
self-production. Importantly, management noted that current
capacity remains insufficient e ven after the Phase 2 ramp, with

demand supported by multiple growth drivers, including 2.5D
advanced packaging, 3D IC, panel-level packaging (PLP), HBM, and
Co-Packaged Optics (CPO). Overall, we reiterate our view that GPTC
is moving beyond a single 2.5D-driven upcycle, with successive

### Page 3

waves of advanced packaging — SoIC, PLP , CPO — extending its growth runway well into
2028 and beyond. Net net, we now model GPTC’s revenue to grow 34%/49%/39% YoY
in 2026E-2028E (vs. 33%/52%/15% YoY previously).
3D IC to drive next phase of growth
On 3D IC, management reiterated that GPTC will remain the major 3D IC wet clean
equipment supplier, anchored by its technology leadership vs. local peers. While some
degree of second-sourcing is to be expected as the technology matures, we continue to

view SoIC as a key driver of content value uplift through 2027/2028, underpinned by
rapid industry capacity expansion and structurally higher equipment ASP vs. CoWoS.
Management characterized 3D packaging as the fastest-growing segment, with 2026
marking the year of initial volume production and 2027/2028 set to see a materially
stronger revenue contribution as 3D IC adoption a
ccelerates. Furthermore, management
also highlighted that 3D IC wet clean equipment enjoys structurally higher GM vs.

2.5D wet clean equipment, providing a more favorable product mix tailwind to overall
corporate GM through 2028. Overall, we view GPTC as a key beneficiar
y of SoIC, and
project SoIC to a ccount for c.50% of GPTC’s equipment revenue in 2027E-28E, with CPO
and future generations of AI chips to drive broader SoIC adoption.
GM expansion ahead on outsourcing ratio reduction and product mix shift
We see a clear path for GPTC’s GM to trend meaningfully higher through 2026-28,

supported by 1) outsourcing ratio reduction — with GPTC’s Phase 2 fab now entering
production, in-house manufacturing capacity is set to expand materially, enabling lower
outsourcing ratio into 2027. 2) product mix shift towards higher-complexity
equipment — 3D IC, panel level-packaging, and CPO brings not only higher dollar
content value but also a richer underlying margin profile, as mor
e demanding wet clean
processes command both stronger pricing and higher GM. Net net, we now model GM to

reach 37.5%/44.1%/47.6% in 2026E-28E (vs. 37.3%/43.4%/46.1% previously), with the
bulk of GM expansion concentrated in 2027 as the in-house production transition
matures and the 3D IC/PLP product mix a
ccelerates.

Earnings changes, valuation and risks
Forecast changes
We revise our 2026E-28E EPS by 1.2%/-7.4%/23.0% mainly as we factor in 1) stronger
2028 demand/capacity outlook, 2) higher GM on more favorable product mix and lower
outsourcing ratio, and 3) higher opex on head count expansion.
Maintain Buy with 12m TP revised up to NT$4,500 from NT$4,000 prior
Following our upward earnings revisions, we raise our 12-month TP to NT$4,500 (from
NT$4,000 previously). Our 12-month TP is based on a target P/E multiple of 35x

(unchanged) applied to our FY2H27E-1H28E EPS (rolled over from 2027E on stronger
demand visibility). Our 35x target P/E continues to be based on an avg. fwd P/E during
the CoWoS ramp-up in mid-2024 to 2025. Our new TP implies 44.5% upside from
current levels, and we maintain our Buy rating on GPTC.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis - GPTC (3131.TWO)
GPTC is one of the premier suppliers of semiconductor wet processing equipment,
primarily utilized in back-end advanced packaging processes. The company
manufactures semiconductor wet processing equipment, including metal etching
equipment and single-wafer cleaning equipment, as well as providing engineering and
installation services to semiconductor manufacturers. GPTC is the main CoWoS wet
clean equipment provider and has 50% of market share at TSMC and near 100% of

market share at ASE/SPIL. GPTC is also the sole provider for SoIC wet clean equipment at
TSMC given its leading technology versus local peers.
We favor GPTC as we expect its revenue/earnings CAGR to further a
ccelerate to
40%/56% in 2025-28E, driven by 1) continued advanced packaging capacity expansion,
and 2) equipment ASP increase amid rising advanced packaging (e.g. SoIC, CPO, FOPLP)
technology complexity. The company is now trading below its AI cycle (2023-2025)

average forward P/E of 29x; however, with proliferation of advanced packaging into
non-AI application and increasing ASP thanks to rising technology complexity, we
continue to see upside potential to its revenue and earnings growth and maintain a Buy
rating on the name.

Valuation: We are Buy rated on GPTC. Our 12m TP of NT$4,500 is based on a target P/E
multiple of 35x (based on avg. fwd P/E during the CoWoS ramp-up in mid-2024 to 2025)

applied to our FY2H27E-1H28E EPS.
Key risks to our views: 1) softer AI/HPC demand, 2) slower adoption of new advanced
packaging technologies, and 3) intensifying competition.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.

Global Research
Micron Technology Inc
LTAs Gain Traction; PT to $1,625 with EPS
Remaining Well >$100
Summary
With LTAs now firmly in place across most of the industry, we are again raising C2027-
2029 estimates and expect EPS to remain comfortably >$100 throughout the period,
with MU generating over $400B in FCF across the same timeframe. We believe the
market will start to put a more “normal” multiple on the stock and  MU will continue to

re-rate higher as more details emerge about the structural changes AI has driven to the
entire memory complex. Our supply chain work on Long Term Agreements (LTAs) across
the memory industry suggests that up to 30% of DDR volumes industry-wide will be
soon locked in at pricing that is just slightly below current levels and these agreements
will allow MU to trade some near-term revenue for demand visibility and a smoother
earnings profile. Consequently, we are raising EPS across C27/28/29E to $155/$167/

$117 (vs. $133/$122/$77 prior) - and considering that investors typically reward stocks
for durability and visibility, we see MU’s EPS remaining >$100 through C2029E as
testament to the sort of lasting, structural change that should support a shift toward a
broader semi multiple. Net, we lift our PT from $535 to $1,625, now based on ~15x
NTM P/E (vs. prior SoTP) on C2029E EPS of $117 (1-yr discounted), and reiterate Buy.
Long Term Agreements (LTAs) - Details and Rationale

In contrast to prior periods where offtake agreements were simply volume based, these
new ‘enhanced’ LTAs now incorporate longer durations, fixed volume commitments and
- most importantly - a partially fixed pricing framework. It is our understanding that
these 'enhanced' LTAs now span over a ~3-5 year period in either a '2+3' structure (two
years of fixed terms, three years variable), or a '3+2' format. In DRAM, we think 20-30%
of industry C2027 DDR bit shipments will now be covered under 'enhanced' LTAs. By

company, we assume 18% SK Hynix, 20% MU, and 30% Samsung – this is contracted
volume with fixed pricing, while pricing will still ‘float’ for the remainder of bits. At this
point, we  believe hyperscalers alone to have currently secured ~60-70% of the industry
Server DDR5 volumes under 'enhanced' LTAs - in other words, guaranteeing MU and
others offtake for this volume. In NAND, a similar pricing structure may be emerging,
with roughly 20% of volumes potentially moving to fixed-volume agreements but

Samsung, SK Hynix, and likely MU seem less willing to commit new cleanroom capacity
to NAND with the same level of certainty as DRAM. In assessing the rationale for
'enhanced' LTAs, we see two primary benefits for memory suppliers: (1) smoother
earnings/revenue profile and higher cross-cycle ROIC, and (2) improved visibility into
now-committed customer demand. On cyclicality, incorporating LTAs into our MU
model suggests these agreements should reduce the magnitude of DDR peak-to-trough

pricing swings by roughly 2x compared to a scenario with no committed volumes or
pricing structure. Even more important, this scenario yields MU EPS of >$100 even out in
C2029E when we do believe DRAM pricing will undergo a fairly significant correction. In
other words, we think MU still earns >$100 even if pricing for the floating portion of

DDR declines by ~50%. Conversely, from the hyperscaler perspective, the rationale for
entering 'enhanced' LTAs appears centered around (1) supply assurance and (2) partial
insulation from further escalation in memory BOM costs. Said differently, hyperscalers
appear increasingly willing to exchange pricing for multi-year supply visibility and greater
predictability around future deployment economics.
Summary of Model Changes

We now expect the DRAM industry to remain undersupplied until at least C2Q28 (vs.
4Q27 prior), and NAND undersupply to last until 4Q27 (was 3Q27). An additional driver
of upside vs. our prior model stems from higher HBM ASP ($/GB) assumptions -
something we highlighted already early in April - as MU/SK/Samsung intend to rebuild a
premium for HBM pricing into C2027, leading us to now model MU HBM ASP up
~50% Y/Y (vs. +35% Y/Y prior) on unchanged MU HBM bit shipment assumptions of

7.78B Gb for C2026 and 12.05B Gb for C2027. Into CQ3:26 our industry model now
also considers the impact of LTAs - as primarily reflected in the magnitude of the
quarterly DDR (up ~8% Q/Q) and NAND (up ~9% Q/Q) industry contract pricing
increase. This implies Q/Q pricing growth now normalizing back toward the low SD%
range after  three, consecutive quarters of pricing increases averaging >50%.
Valuation: PT to $1,625 (from $535)
We raise our price target from $535 to $1,625 based on a ~15x NTM P/E multiple

(noting we see no reason why MU should trade a whole lot differently than NVDA in
terms of P/E), which is in line with the 3-yr average, and applied to our ~$117 C2029E
EPS and discounted back to C2028E using a COE of ~12%. Importantly, we anchor on
C2029E EPS as we believe it best reflects MU’s through-cycle earnings power under LTAs
because, by that point, our model assumes a moderate memory downcycle, albeit with
LTAs buoying the company’s earnings base.

### Page 12

Micron Technology Inc26 May 2026 ab 12
Micron Technology Inc UBS Research
ValuatioN
We raise our price target from $535 to $1,625. In doing so, we shift our valuation
framework to a NTM P/E methodology from a prior SoTP approach. We believe this
change is warranted as Long Term Agreements (LTAs) are fundamentally changing the
earnings profile of Micron, driving a structural re-rating of the shares. Specifically, while
MU has historically traded as a hyper-cyclical name, we now see LTAs effectively

anchoring demand and pricing, which should materially reduce earnings volatility and
enhance visibility. As a result, we believe MU increasingly merits a valuation in line with
broader Semiconductor peers, rather than at a discounted, hyper cycle-driven multiple.
Net, our price target is based on a ~15x NTM P/E multiple (vs. our prior ~5x NTM P/E
multiple implied by our SoTP valuation), applied to our ~$117 C2029E EPS and
discounted back to C2028E using a COE of ~11.8%. Importantly, we anchor on C2029E

EPS as we believe it best reflects MU’s through-cycle earnings power under LTAs as,  by
that point, our model assumes a moderate memory downcycle, albeit with LTAs buoying
the company’s earnings base.

Upside/Downside Spectrum
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Source: UBS estimates
UPSIDE ($1,850): We assume 29E HBM ASP to decrease by -6% and HBM bit to grow by
18% Y/Y. Excluding HBM,   we see DRAM ASP to go down by -42% while DRAM bit to
grow by 2% Y/Y. For NAND, we assume C29E NAND ASP to decrease by  -52%  and
NAND bit to increase by 13% Y/Y. By applying a ~16.0x P/E multiple, resulting in an

upside case of $1,850.
BASE ($1,625): We assume 29E HBM ASP to increase by 5% and HBM bit to grow by
30% Y/Y. Excluding HBM,   we see DRAM ASP to decrease  by -45% while DRAM bit to
grow by 14% Y/Y. For NAND, we expect C29E ASP to decrease by -56% and NAND bit to
grow by 9% Y/Y. By applying a ~15.0x P/E multiple, resulting in a base case of $1,625.
DOWNSIDE ($250): We assume 29E HBM ASP to decrease by -43% and HBM bit to
decline by -33% Y/Y. Excluding HBM,   we see DRAM ASP to down by -70% while DRAM

bit to decrease by -49% Y/Y. For NAND, we assume C29E ASP to decrease by  -77% and
NAND   bit to decrease by -45% Y/Y. By applying a ~11.0x P/E multiple, resulting in a
downside case of $250.

Company Description
Micron Technology Inc. designs and manufactures semiconductor memory and storage
solutions. Micron operates in four segments. The Compute and Networking Business Unit
serves the client, cloud server, enterprise, graphics, and networking markets. The Mobile

Business Unit addresses the needs of  the mobile handset and tablet markets. The Storage
business unit sells into cloud, enterprise and client storage markets. The Embedded Business
Unit sells memory to automotive, industrial, and consumer customers. Micron was founded in
1978 and is headquartered in Boise, ID.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Micron Technology Inc (MU): to arrive at our PT we use a valuation method solely based on
NTM P/E. Memory has been fairly correlated to global GDP . Therefore from a macro

perspective there could be potential downside to our PT should economic conditions worsen
and leading to demand destruction. Should ASP declines be more severe than our
expectations, there could be more downside in the stock. Scaling both DRAM and NAND is
highly complex and risks on the Technology front abound. There could be further downside
should technology migration be slower than we expect. On the upside, while we expect ASPs
to decline on account of incremental supply addition, any delays to the timelines could

moderate the growth of supply and create an upward bias to ASPs and MU. Other risks
include a incremental reliance of AI on memory driving up content in Data Center servers and
devices (PCs, SPs, etc).

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Unimicron  | Asia Pacific
Very strong preliminary April
profits
Stay OW: Unaudited preliminary April numbers announced by Unimicron imply
significant upside to our and the Street's 2Q and likely full-year numbers as well, all
else being equal. We don't have visibility into how much of this beat is driven by
non-op gains, but we are of the view that better pricing, mix, and utilization rate is
driving some of the beat, particularly in ABF and BT substrates. Unimicron reported

prelim March profits
last month with net margin at 8.7% only, implying a 1170bps q/
q improvement for the month of April. Our supply chain checks suggest that ABF
substrate pricing adjustments were more meaningful in 2Q, which aligns with
management's comments on more prompt pricing adjustments in response to raw
material cost inflation. We reiterate our OW on the stock, as we believe we are in
the early innings of an AI-driven up-cycle, with under-supply kicking in from CY27

onwards (
see here for our recent upgrade report and a more recent report in which
we raised our price target  and earnings estimates). We prefer Unimicron to NYPCB
and Zhen Ding over a 12-month view.

TWSE.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn&#39;t require substrate
(e.g., CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Zhen Ding (4958.TW)

Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 10%, a medium-
term growth rate of 15% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Higher-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones
n Better-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Share gains on higher margin F-PCB pieces
Risks to Downside
n Worse-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Lower-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones

n Worse-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Increasing competition from Chinese peers, intensifying pricing pressure
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
WNC (6285.TW): 800G switch in expansion; Buy with TP up to NT$364
28 May 2026 | 2:26PM HKT
WNC is benefiting from networking technology migrations. As a major ODM supplier
of network communications solutions with long-term experience, WNC enables rapid
shipment of new designs for its customers. We remain positive on WNC, riding on the
networking switch product expansion, including the Enterprise 800G switch
(contributing revenues in 2H26) and OCS switch (long-term opportunities), the

accelerating adoption of LEO satellite networks (Read more), and the WiFi-7
adoption cycle that is driving up replacement demand. We raise our 12-month target
price by 14.5% to NT$364.0 and maintain our Buy rating.
800G switch in expansion. As a leading ODM for networking equipment, WNC will
start mass shipment of 800G switch in 2H26. The 800G switch will be an ODM
product for branded customers, targeting enterprise clients as the major end users.

This supports our positive view on rising networking demand along with AI
computing, where 800G high-speed products have already expanded applications
from CSPs to enterprises. With ongoing networking technology migrations, we
expect 800G switch demand to continue into 2027 and may expand to 1.6T in the
future. Apart from the 800G switch, OCS switches are also a long term opportunity
(link
), as they are also innovative networking products that will greatly increase data

transmission efficienc y and in our view have the potential to become a mainstream
form factor in the future. We are positive that the continuous development of
advanced networking solutions will support WNC’s growth along with the ramping
trend of networking advancements in AI data centers.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at
https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

WT Microelectronics Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
Outlook by segments: Data center and communication  segments remain the
primary growth driver with strong revenue visibility into 2H. Industrial demonstrates
a structural recovery with projected ~20% Y/Y growth for 2026, driven by power
component demand. Auto segment shows recovery momentum in Europe and
Americas, while the Asian markets remains sluggish, especially China market, and

overall moderate growth is expected in 2Q. Consumer/PC segment demand visibility
is low following customer pull-in in 2H25, Mobile phone segment is still expected to
experience seasonal uplifts in 2H26. 2Q financials likely to fall at the mid-point of
guidance (
link).
Future Electronics currently accounts for around 10% revenue contribution due to
WT's strong data center growth. Currently, Future's ROWC is below corporate
average.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
WT Microelectronics Co. Ltd. (3036.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 12.6% (2% risk-free rate, 11.8%
risk premium, 0.9 beta), a medium-term growth rate of 10% and a long-term growth rate of
3%.
Risks to Upside
n Margin trends up even amid low-margin AI business.
n Global semi inventory drops, reflecting strong mobile device demand.
n Market share expands.

Risks to Downside
n Margin trends down dramatically amid slower-than-expected AI-semi development.
n Global semi inventory rises, reflecting poor mobile/PC device demand.
n Market share lost.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

2026.05.21 
吳昆潮 C00269@ibfs.com.tw 
2618.TT 長榮航 
 油價變數大，獲利承壓

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
長榮航空成立於 1989 年，隸屬長榮集團，航線遍及亞洲、歐洲、美洲、大洋洲等全
球逾 60 個城市。機隊以波音 777、787 及空中巴士 A321neo 為主。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 38 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，38 
【大盤指數/股價】40021 / 34.3

➢ 燃油成本下降，1Q26 獲利優於預期  
長榮航 1Q26 營收 605.16 億元，QoQ+4.98%，YoY+10.14%，稅後淨
利 82.68 億元，QoQ+8.10%，YoY+39.43%，EPS 1.53 元。1Q26 客運
適逢農曆春節及寒假，出國旅遊需求旺盛，客運搭載人數 367.1 萬人，
YoY+10.1%，客運營收 402.9 億元，YoY+10.7%，平均載客率 84.1%。
貨運方面 ， 受惠AI 伺服器及半導體設備持續出貨 ，1Q26 貨運營收 132.4
億元，YoY+4.1%。雖然 2 月底中東戰事引發油價飆升，但 3 月份中油
航空燃油牌價尚未完全反映成本壓力，僅部分國際線於 3 月中下旬受到
影響，對 1Q26 整體成本影響有限，長榮航 1Q26 燃油均價 85.56 美元
/桶，YoY-5.97%，燃油佔營運成本比重維持在 30%。 
➢ 2Q26 燃油成本倍增，單季獲利接近損平 
中東戰事導致油價大漲，中油 4 月、5 月航空燃油牌價平均在 205 美元
/桶，較1Q26 平均 94 美元/桶上漲 118%， 布蘭特原油5 月平均仍維持
在 106 美元/桶，預期 2Q26 航空燃油平均成本將較 1Q26 倍增。投研
部預估長榮航 2Q26 航空燃油均價約 180 美元/桶 ， 燃油成本驟增至260
億元，雖然貨運轉嫁能力佳，而客運燃油附加費僅能反映 15%~35%不
等的成本，高燃油成本對獲利的衝擊將在 2Q26 完全反映。投研部預估
長榮航 2Q26 營收 625.90 億元，QoQ+3.43%，YoY+13.17%，稅後淨
利 5.64 億元，QoQ-93.18%，YoY-91.58%，EPS 0.10 元。 
➢ 中東戰爭膠著，上修全年航空燃油價格 
目前中東戰爭陷入膠著，油價居高不下，儘管我們仍預期伊朗終將開放
荷姆茲海峽，原油供給壓力會逐漸紓緩，但油價下跌速度比預期慢，因
此投研部上修長榮航 2026 年航空燃油均價至 131 美元/桶，相較 2025
年燃油均價 86.7 美元/桶增加 51%。 
➢ 預估長榮航 2026 年 EPS 3.30 元，建議區間操作，目標價 38 元 
投研部預估長榮航 2026 年營收 2,431.80 億元，YoY+10.37%，稅後淨
利 178.46 億元 ，YoY-31.75%，EPS 3.30 元 ， 儘管航空客貨量需求穩定 ，
但中東戰事及油價不確定性仍高，燃油成本壓力大， 維持長榮航區間操
作的投資建議，目標價 38 元(2026F PB 1.4X)。

元，QoQ+8.10%，YoY+39.43%，EPS 1.53 元。1Q26 客運適逢農曆春節及寒假，
出國旅遊需求旺盛，客運搭載人數 367.1 萬人，YoY+10.1%，客運營收 402.9 億元，
YoY+10.7%， 平均載客率84.1%。 貨運方面 ， 受惠AI 伺服器及半導體設備持續出貨 ，
1Q26 貨運營收 132.4 億元，YoY+4.1%。雖然 2 月底中東戰事引發油價飆升，但 3
月份中油航空燃油牌價尚未完全反映成本壓力，僅部分國際線於 3 月中下旬受到影
響 ， 對1Q26 整體成本影響有限 ， 長榮航1Q26 燃油均價 85.56 美元/桶，YoY-5.97%，
燃油佔營運成本比重維持在 30%。

全球經濟穩健成長，客貨運需求展望仍佳 
IMF 預估 2026 年、2027 年全球經濟成長率 3.1%、3.2%，其中亞洲新興經濟體經濟
成長率優於歐美，在出境旅遊、外貿與科技投資的共同拉動下，對航空客貨運的需求
強勁，民眾可支配所得提升，促使區域航線與跨境商旅需求持續增加，平均載客率屢
創新高，多數維持在 80%~90%的歷史高檔位置。在貨運方面，AI 投資浪潮成為核心
引擎，全球 AI 伺服器與高階晶片的組裝和製造，帶動半導體需求貨運暢旺，高價值、
高時效性的精密電子零組件與電商急件，拉高貨運單位收益(yield)，長榮航 2026 年
1~4 月累計載貨 26.9 萬噸 ，YoY+2.17%， 但單位收益平均12.49 元/噸公里 ， 較2025
年同期 11.59 元/噸公里成長 7.76%。

2Q26 燃油成本倍增，單季獲利接近損平 
中東戰事導致油價大漲，中油 4 月、5 月航空燃油牌價平均在 205 美元/桶，較1Q26
平均 94 美元/桶上漲 118%， 布蘭特原油5 月平均仍維持在 106 美元/桶 ， 預期2Q26
航空燃油平均成本將較 1Q26 倍增。長榮航 1Q26 航空燃油均價 85.56 美元/桶，燃
油佔營運成本比重 30%，約133 億元 ， 投研部預估長榮航2Q26 航空燃油均價約 180
美元/桶，若以用油量 460 萬桶/季推算，預估 2Q26 燃油成本驟增至 260 億元，雖然
貨運轉嫁能力佳，而客運燃油附加費僅能反映 15-35%不等的成本，加上多數機票已
在事先預訂，致使 2Q26 機票實際反映漲幅的比例不高，高燃油成本對獲利的衝擊將
在 2Q26 完全反映。投研部預估長榮航 2Q26 營收 625.90 億元，QoQ+3.43%，
YoY+13.17%，稅後淨利 5.64 億元，QoQ-93.18%，YoY-91.58%，EPS 0.10 元。 
中東戰爭膠著，上修全年航空燃油價格 
目前中東戰爭陷入膠著，油價居高不下，儘管我們仍預期伊朗終將開放荷姆茲海峽，
原油供給壓力會逐漸紓緩，但油價下跌速度比預期慢，因此投研部上修長榮航 2026
年航空燃油均價至 131 美元/桶，相較 2025 年燃油均價 86.7 美元/桶增加 51%。 
預估長榮航 2026 年 EPS 3.30 元，建議區間操作，目標價 38 元 
投研部預估長榮航 2026 年營收 2,431.80 億元，YoY+10.37%，稅後淨利 178.46 億
元，YoY-31.75%，EPS 3.30 元，儘管航空客貨量需求穩定，但中東戰事及油價不確
定性仍高，燃油成本壓力大，維持長榮航區間操作的投資建議，目標價 38 元(2026F 
PB 1.4X)。

2026 年展望不錯

永豐觀點 
營運展望樂觀，營收將逐季向上。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議轉買進：1Q 獲利表現佳，因日本、歐洲等市場在矽水膠等高階產品持續
放量下，今年營運展望樂觀，營收將逐季向上，加上目前本益比12 倍，評價不高，
股價有上漲空間，調升評等至買進，目標價 372 元( 15 X 2026 EPS )。

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：晶碩為台灣專業隱形眼鏡製造廠商， 營運模式上採自有品牌和代工雙軌
併行 ，1Q26 年品牌和代工營收比重為 17.8%及 82.2%，營收依區域佔比分別為其
他亞洲 78.24%、台灣 14.68%及其他 7.09%，其他亞洲中，日本約佔 65%，中國
15%，台灣競爭同業為海昌、精華光學、望隼、視陽等，國際競爭同業為嬌生、博
士倫等。 
費用控管得宜，1Q 獲利表現優於預期：晶碩 1Q 營收 18.9 億元(-2.3%QoQ、
+18.3%YoY)，毛利率 53.3%，稅後淨利 4.72 億元(+10.2%QoQ、-3.1%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 6.05 元，表現優於預期，主因費用控管得宜，拉升1Q 營利率至 28.8%，
較過去幾季約落於 25-27%高，另單季稅率 15.6%，也低於原預期之 20%。 
日本及歐洲市場矽水膠放量，營運展望樂觀，2026 年預估營收將成長 10-15%：
就各市場目前市況，日本部分，除矽水膠日拋訂單持續成長外，高階品產品包括散
光及多焦系列需求也再增加，彩片部分， 除既有水膠品項因應客戶需求持續推出新
圖紋外，高階產品也有彩片系列商品上市下，預估日本市場今年營收將成長 15-
20%，動能明確，中國部分，因陸廠積極擴產，故在價格競爭力下滑下 (和當地廠
相比，晶碩加計稅與運費等成本，產品報價價差近20%)，2025 年營運較為辛苦，
因應此，產能端晶碩除在中國南通廠進行適度擴產外，越南廠 2H27 也會接棒投
產，因越南輸中國地區免關稅加上運輸上陸運即可下，可有效縮小報價差異，另產
品端，2-3Q26 矽水膠等高階產品將取證，產品定位有機會突破價格廝殺區塊下，
中國市場今年將力拼持平至小幅成長，歐洲市場則在矽水膠系列產品項更趨完整
下，客戶訂單量持續增加中，台灣部分，除自有品牌外，也新增韓系品牌代工下，
預估今年營收將可維持穩健。整體而言，各市場營運展望多穩健至正向，預估晶碩
2026 年營收可達 80.1 億元(+13.7%YoY)。 
產能配置規畫 ：目前月產能約 1.05-1.1 億片 ， 預計至年底將擴充至1.15-1.2 億片 ，
配置上，隨矽水膠出貨量增，大溪廠將持續擴充矽水膠產能，龜山廠則維持現狀，
越南廠則預計 2H27 投產，第一期月產能設定在每月 0.1-0.15 億片。 
財務預估：晶碩 2025 年營收 70.4 億元(+3.3%YoY)，毛利率 52.3%，稅後淨利
16.3 億元(-11.1%YoY)，稅後 EPS 20.87 元，預計配發現金股利 10 元，目前殖利
率約 3.3%。預估 2026 年營收 80.1 億元(+13.7%YoY)，毛利率因經濟規模與匯率
友善 ， 由53.7%上修至 54.5%， 稅後淨利19.4 億元(+18.7%YoY)， 稅後EPS 24.86
元(原估 22.37 元)。

1

6957 裕慶-KY(Horizon)

報告日期： 2026/05/22 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.51 元 
在主要客戶 1Q26 積極出貨帶動，裕慶 KY 1Q26 營收金額 10.08
億元， YoY+28.64% ，QoQ-12.37% ，受到 1~2 月仍有高額關稅影
響，營業利益 1.01 億元， YoY+65.33% ，QoQ-68.78% ，稅後盈
餘 0.83 億元， YoY+29.03% ，QoQ-68.52% ，稅後 EPS 1.51 元。 
二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.48 元 
裕慶 KY 04/2026 營收金額 3.68 億元 ，YoY+53.31% ，MoM-26.6% 。 
在商用展示架客戶訂單持續回溫，預估裕慶 KY 2Q26 營收金額
11.34 億元， YoY+40.9% ，QoQ+12.5% ，預估營業利益 2.38 億
元， YoY+516.67% ，QoQ+135.4% ，預估稅後盈餘 1.93 億元，
YoY+414.86% ，QoQ+134.08% ，預估稅後 EPS 3.48 元。  
三 . 預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43 億元，
YoY+24.64% 
商用展示架 2026 年營運成長動能： 1，美國家居裝修零售量販店
在自動灑水展示架出貨量增加，預估貢獻 2026 年營收金額 8~8.5
億元， YoY+42~51% 。2，美國折扣零售百貨持續增加原有門市汰
舊換新訂單，加上新店展示架訂單，預期貢獻 2026 年營收金額
6~6.5 億元， YoY+130~170% 。3，美國跨國零售公司，在零售賣
場的展示架翻新計畫，新據點展示架，加上客戶將商示架訂單集
中至裕慶 ， 預計2026 年貢獻營收金額 4.5~5 億元 ，YoY+42~59% 。
預估商用展示架 2026 年營收 43 億元， YoY+24.64% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
裕慶 KY 2026 年在商用展示架新客戶如美國家居裝修零售量販
店，美國折扣零售百貨與新客戶訂單挹注，帶動裕慶 KY 2026 年
獲利恢成長態勢 。 宏遠預估裕慶 KY 2026 年營收金額 46.00 億元 ，
YoY+20.3% ，預估營業利益 9.08 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後
盈餘 7.22 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。  
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 208 元 
觀察裕慶 2026 年 ROE25% ，PER 12.3X ，ROE-PER 評價偏低，  
1Q~2Q26 營運呈現年增，有助評價提升，故投資建議買進，目標
價 208 元。 (16X2026 年稅後 EPS )

2 
貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
裕慶金屬成立於 1998 年，資本額 5.55 億元，並於 2024 年掛牌
上市。裕慶早期主要以展示架產品，提供全球大型零售通路商，
作為商品陳設。之後更跨足服飾、飲料、飯店、製鞋、油漆、傢
俱等全球領導品牌的商用展示架；此外，居家生活用的電動床，
其內部精密的金屬結構與製造，也得到客戶的認同，成為全球獨
家製造廠。更立基於此，近年投入資源，透過電子設備提供更有
價值的訊息，為居家生活提供更完整的加值服務。

圖一、裕慶公司沿革

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二. 投資架構與生產基地概況  
開曼裕慶旗下有五間子公司。台灣分公司位於台灣南投，主要以
營運總部，生產製造，南投廠占整體產能約 10~15% 。郁智康，
主要以電子裝置零組件開發為主，主要用於智能電動床機構件。
蘇州裕慶主要為生產製造為主，蘇州廠占整體產能約 85~90% 。
環宇木業，為裕慶 2017 年收購，主要以木質生產基地為主，搭配
裕慶原有金屬材質商用展示架。美國芝加哥子公司  Beeline 之設
計團隊與在地倉儲服務。

圖二、裕慶投資組織架構

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
裕慶主要從事各類材質商用展示架與智能電動床機件之研發，生
產及銷售，展場設計服務，與居家健康管理智能模組研發等。

圖三、裕慶主要產品—商用展示架與智能電動床機構件

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43 億元， YoY+24.64%

商用展示架產業具有高度競爭市場，其競爭情況受多個因素影
響，包括生產要素的價格波動、產品與消費需求波動、供應鏈變
化、創新和全球化等不同因素，環環相扣。裕慶生產各類材質的
商用展示架，從設計、工程結構以及製造多年經驗，客戶主要為
北美知名品牌或零售業者 ， 客戶包括 Adidas、 BOOT BARN 、Coca 
Cola、DICKS、HOME DEPOT 、H&M、KOHLS 、LOWES 、MUJI 、 
TARGET 、UNIQLO 與 Walmart 等。

公司研究報告

4

商用展示架主要市場在美國，其中中國出口至美國關稅課徵高額
關稅，加上台幣升值影響，許多客戶訂單呈現減少情況，但其中
不乏逆勢成長客戶，包括： 1，美國家居裝修零售量販店在自動灑
水展示架出貨量持續增加，加上拖拉車訂單挹注，貢獻裕慶 2025
年營收金額 5.6 億元， YoY+102% 。2，美國跨國零售公司，在零
售賣場的展示架翻新計畫 ， 貢獻裕慶 2025 年營收金額 3.15 億元 ，
呈現倍數成長。 3，美國折扣零售百貨集團訂單己於 4Q24 小量出
貨， 2025 年開始放量， 2025 年貢獻裕慶營收金額 1.56 億元，呈
現倍增。商用展示架 2025 年營收金額 34.50 億元， YoY+4.2% 。

商用展示架 2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，美國家居裝修零售量
販店在自動灑水展示架出貨量持續增加 ， 預估貢獻裕慶 2026 年營
收金額 8~8.5 億元， YoY+42~51% 。2，美國折扣零售百貨持續增
加原有門市汰舊換新訂單，另外也有新店展示架訂單，預期貢獻
2026 年營收金額 6~6.5 億元， YoY+130~170% 。3，美國跨國零
售公司，在零售賣場的展示架翻新計畫，新據點展示架，加上客
戶將商示架訂單集中至裕慶，預計 2026 年貢獻裕慶營收金額
4.5~5 億元， YoY+42~59% 。4，裕慶新業務方面，己取國內某居
家門鎖公司 60% 股權，初期 2026 年客戶將以大型零售通路與電
商通路為主，預估貢獻 2026 年營收金額 0.5~1 億元。綜合上述，
宏遠預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43.00 億元 ，YoY+24.64%。

預估智能電動床機構件 2026 年營收金額 3 億元， YoY-19.8%

智能電動床主要消費市場在於歐美地區，智能電動床最初鎖定消
費者為行動不便的老年人口，消費族群絕大多數為 50 歲以上人
口，但隨著消費生活品質提升，消費結構的升級與業者的推廣，
越來愈多年輕族群開始購買智能電動床，根據國際睡眠品質協會
統計，智能電動床美國地區銷售量由 2016 年的 105.09 萬張成長
至 2022 年的 207.83 萬張，年複合成長率 12.04% 。隨著互聯網
普及與消費習慣提升後，智能電動床將逐漸吞食一般床的市場份
額，其市場仍具有成長空間。裕慶智能電動床機構件主要客戶為
美國 Leggett ＆Platt 。

裕慶 2025 年，智能電動床客戶在美國關稅因素，減少訂單，受到
主要客戶訂單減少影響 ， 預估智能電動床機構件 2025 年營收金額
3.74 億元， YoY-46.88%

預期 2026 年，在美國關稅成本增加，導致民間消費通膨，民眾將
消費將以必需品為主，非必要品將會被排擠，恐不利智能電動床
銷售 ， 預估智能電動床機構件 2026 年營收金額 3 億元 ，YoY-19.8%

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 5 月 22 日

1 群益投顧 
昇陽半導體 (8028 TT) 評估赴美設廠 。先 進 製 程 帶 動 再 生 晶 圓 需 求 用 量 提 升，昇 陽 半 導
體 有 大 規 模 的 擴 廠 ，2026~2027 年營運正向，投資建議維持 Buy ，目標價 310 元。

研 究 員 ： 莊 禮 誠   
dan.chuang@cim.capital.com.tw

晶 圓 代 工 廠 、 記 憶 體 廠 擴 廠 帶 動 再 生 晶 圓 用 量：  
昇 陽 半 導 體 05/21 召 開 股 東 會 ， 總 經 理 蔡 幸 川 表 示 ，AI 帶 動 再 生 晶 圓 需 求
「 非 常 強 烈 」 ，主 要 來 自 晶 圓 代 工 與 記 憶 體 大 廠 持 續 擴 廠，加 上 先 進 製 程 單
位 產 出 所 需 再 生 晶 圓 用 量 持 續 增 加 ， 推 升 整 體 需 求 快 速 成 長 。 
蔡 幸 川 指 出 ， 目 前 客 戶 擴 產 速 度「 比 預 期 來 得 更 快 」， 該 公 司 已 同 步 對 台 灣
與 美 國 廠 積 極 備 貨 ， 預 估 2H26 至 2027 年 將 進 入 拉 貨 高 峰 期 。 
昇 陽 半 導 體 評 估 去 美 國 設 廠：  
目 前 客 戶 美 國 廠 需 求 非 常 大，市 場 甚 至 傳 出 未 來 可 能 新 增 11 至 12 座 新 廠，
現 階 段 已 有 2 至 3 座 開 始 運 作。昇 陽 半 導 體 目 前 仍 由 台 灣 供 貨 至 美 國，但 已
著 手 評 估 赴 美 設 廠 ， 未 來 將 朝 在 地 供 應 方 向 發 展 。 
AI 晶 片 帶 動 晶 圓 薄 化 需 求：  
除 再 生 晶 圓 外 ， 該 公 司 晶 圓 薄 化 業 務 也 受 惠AI 伺 服 器 高 功 率 能 源 管 理 需 求
快 速 成 長 。 蔡 幸 川 表 示 ， 客 戶 已 切 入 NVIDIA(NVDA US) 下 世 代 AI 晶 片 電
源 管 理 供 應 鏈 ， 帶 動 薄 化 需 求 倍 數 增 加 ， 未 來 除AI 外 ， 車 用 市 場 預 期 1 至
2 年 內 也 將 恢 復 成 長 動 能 。 
預 計 再 生 晶 圓 未 來 月 產 能 逾 百 萬 片：  
產 能 方 面 ， 昇 陽 半 導 體 2025 年 底 月 產 能 已 達 85 萬 片 ， 2026 年 仍 將 持 續 擴
充 ， 未 來 主 要 成 長 動 能 來 自 台 中 港 全 自 動 化 新 廠 ， 預 計2027 年 底 投 產 ， 單
廠 設 計 產 能 達 60 萬 片 。 蔡 幸 川 強 調 ， 該 公 司 不 會 因 需 求 熱 絡 而 調 漲 價 格 ，
仍 將 透 過 製 程 精 進 與 成 本 下 降 維 持 高 毛 利 率 策 略 。 
昇 陽 半 導 體 2025 年於 5 奈 米 及 3 奈 米 世 代 製 程 再 生 晶 圓 市 占 率 顯 著 提 升 ，
並 同 步 延 伸 布 局 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈 ， 提 前 卡 位2 奈 米 以 下 先 進 製 程 需 求 。 
2025 年 營 收 創 新 高：  
2025 年 營 收 達 45.10 億 元 ， 年 增 26.97 ％ ， 連 續 五 年 改 寫 新 高 ； 受 惠 高 階 產
品 組 合 優 化 ， 營 業 利 益 年 增 81.39 ％至 9.70 億 元 ， 顯 示 公 司 營 運 已 由 過 往
「 產 能 驅 動 」 逐 步 轉 向 「 價 值 驅 動 」 。 
公 司 投 入 SiC 的 解 決 方 案 ， 用 來 提 升 封 裝 時 的散 熱 效 率 與 穩 定 性 ：  
目 前 研 發 重 點 聚 焦「 先 進 製 程 測 試 晶 圓 」 、 「12 吋 功 率 IC 晶 圓 薄 化 」 、 「 晶
片 散 熱 解 決 方 案 」 及 「 製 造 工 廠 AI 化 」 四 大 方 向 。 
其 中，針 對 AI 伺 服 器 與 HPC 晶 片 先 進 封 裝 所 面 臨 的 高 熱 能 與 翹 曲 問 題，該
公 司 正 積 極 投 入 SiC 乘 載 晶 圓 解 決 方 案 ， 利 用 碳 化 矽 高 導 熱 與 高 機 械 強 度
特 性 ， 提 升 散 熱 效 率 與 封 裝 穩 定 性 。

2026~2027 年 營 運 正 向 ， 投 資 建 議 維 持 Buy ， 目 標 價 310 元。

### Page 3

產業速報

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

瀚荃 1Q26 EPS 為 1.16 元，低於預期，主因原物料成本上揚、中
國電鍍成本跳升，以及台幣升值造成業外匯損。展望未來，AI 伺
服器電力架構將由傳統 PSU 逐步朝 HVDC、Sidecar、BBU/CBU 及 
DC/DC 降壓模組等架構發展，推升板端連接器單機用量、產品規格
與 ASP，瀚荃可望持續受惠 AI 電力架構升級。研究部預估 2026 
年及 2027 年 EPS 分別為 6.78 元（+57.0% YoY，原估 7.16 元）
及 8.64 元（+27.4% YoY，原估 8.92 元）。基於（1）AI 伺服器
電力架構升級勢在必行，將持續推升板端連接器用量、產品規格與 
ASP；（2）AI Power 過去三年營收 CAGR 達 68%–70%， 預估2026–
2027 年將再分別成長 58.7% / 29.1% YoY，成長具延續性；（3）
東莞新廠與泰國新廠陸續開出 ， 將強化高頻高速與大電流電源產品
供應能力，支撐 AI 電源客戶後續需求，研究部看好瀚荃將持續掌
握 AI 伺服器電力架構升級商機，維持瀚荃評價並下調目標價至
135 元(2026 年及 20XPER 評價)，維持買進評等。

重點摘要： 
1. 1Q26 本業符合預期，惟業外匯損使獲利低於預期 
瀚荃 1Q26 營收為 9.1 億元（+2.0% QoQ、+23.3% YoY），毛利率
為 38.0%（-1.1ppt QoQ、+3.9ppt YoY），低於預期的 39.2%，主
要受到原物料成本上揚、筆電復甦力道低於預期，以及中國電鍍成
本跳升影響 ， 其中電鍍報價上漲約 18%， 對整體毛利率造成約 1 至 
2 個百分點壓力。但 1Q26 伺服器與網通營收占比已提升至 44%，
其中 AI Power 占比達 22%，高毛利產品比重提升帶動產品組合明
顯轉佳，使毛利率仍較去年同期明顯改善。1Q26 營業費用為 2.16 
億元 （+16.0% YoY） ， 主因公開收購全與昕 15% 股權產生近 2,000 
萬元財務顧問費及相關作業成本，低於研究部預估的 2.28 億元；
本業利益為 1.3 億元（+25.8% QoQ、+94.7% YoY），大致符合預
期 。 不過 ， 業外受台幣升值影響產生匯兌損失 ， 單季業外淨損約1100
萬元，致 1Q26 EPS 為 1.16 元（-22.5% QoQ、+34.3% YoY），低
於預期的 1.31 元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

圖一、瀚荃 2025-4Q27F 產品組合預估

資料來源：瀚荃，統一投顧整理

2. 2Q26 營收動能優於預期 ， 惟成本壓力延續 ， 下修毛利率與 EPS 
預估 
瀚荃 4 月營收為 3.72 億元（+11.8% MoM、+21.6% YoY），營收
達成率 38%，表現優於預期。主要係原先公司對網通與通用型伺服
器業務展望保守，全年成長僅約 10% YoY，但網通及通用型伺服器
需求優於預期，故研究部將該業務全年成長上修至 40% YoY。基於 
4 月營收優於預期、伺服器與網通業務優於預期，研究部將 2Q26 
營收預估由 9.80 億元上修至 10.57 億元（+16.2% QoQ、+24.3% 
YoY） 。不過，考量中國電鍍費用大幅跳升，且短期金屬、塑膠料等
原物料價格仍維持高檔，將持續壓抑毛利率表現，因此將 2Q26 毛
利率預估由 39.6% 下修至 38.8%（+0.8ppt QoQ、+1.3ppt YoY）。
單季 EPS 預估由 1.75 元下修至 1.66 元（+43.3% QoQ、+233.7% 
YoY）。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

3. AI 電源架構升級趨勢確立 
隨 AI 伺服器功耗與機櫃密度快速提升，電力架構正由傳統 in-
rack 配電逐步朝 power shelf、BBU/CBU、DC/DC 降壓模組及 HVDC 
發展，帶動電源系統從單一 PSU 供電走向更複雜的分層式電力架
構。過去傳統伺服器 PSU 瓦數多落在 3kW 左右，OCP 架構已提升
至 11–12kW，而下一代 Rubin 架構 PSU 瓦數更上看 18.5kW，代
表伺服器內部必須在更有限空間中承載更高電流 、 更高熱負載與更
高可靠度要求。此一趨勢將直接拉升板端連接器需求，因為 BBU、
CBU 與 DC/DC 降壓模組均需配置更多控制板、功率元件與被動元
件，進而帶動 PCB 板數與連接器用量增加。同時，高電流、高耐
熱、高密度與高可靠度規格升級，也使新世代連接器 ASP 較舊產
品成長數倍，形成「用量增加」與「ASP提升」的雙重成長動能。

圖二、瀚荃 2025-4Q27F 產品組合預估

資料來源：瀚荃，統一投顧整理

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

4. AI Power 放量推升產品組合優化，伺服器與網通成為未來兩
年成長主軸  
瀚荃未來數年最具成長性的業務已明確轉向 AI Power 與伺服器
網通。1Q26 伺服器與網通營收占比已提升至 44%，其中 AI Power 
占比達 22%， 相較 2023 年約 5%、2024 年約 8%、2025 年約 18%，
過去三年 AI Power 營收複合成長率約達 68%–70%。展望未來，
隨 AI 伺服器電力架構由傳統 PSU 逐步朝 HVDC 架構發展，板端
連接器需求將受惠單機用量增加、規格升級與 ASP 提升，其中部
分新世代產品 ASP 較舊產品提高 2–3 倍，甚至達 4–5 倍。受
惠 AI Power 放量，以及網通與通用型伺服器需求優於預期，預估
伺服器與網通業務未來兩年營收將分別成長 51.3% / 24.3% YoY，
營收佔比分別達 46% 與接近51% (2026 / 2027 年 AI Power 占
比提升至 24% / 28%) ；且該業務毛利率優於公司平均，將持續帶
動產品組合優化。此外，東莞新廠預計 1Q27 完工驗收，將定位為
高頻高速與大電流電源產品研發製造中心；泰國新廠則預計 2026 
年底開出，以就近服務 AI 電源客戶，支撐 2027 年後成長動能延
續。

5. 工業電子為第二成長動能，NB 與車用則保守看待 
除 AI Power 與伺服器網通外，瀚荃非伺服器業務以工業電子表現
相對穩健，受惠工業控制、智能倉儲及客製化線束需求延續，且產
品規格較客製化，具備較佳成本轉嫁能力，預期 2026 年仍可維持
雙位數成長，成為公司第二成長動能。相較之下，NB 業務受中國
供應鏈競爭與降價壓力影響，毛利率仍為公司最低，後續將透過蘇
州產線移往重慶降低製造成本，全年營收預估與2025年持平。車用
電子則受歐洲車市疲弱及中國電動車供應鏈競爭加劇影響 ， 短期成
長有限，公司策略以維持既有產品線及保留新能源、機器人等未來
應用彈性為主；其他業務因終端需求分散且缺乏明確成長主軸，預
期小幅衰退。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

6. 上修 2026–2027 年營收預估，惟成本壓力使毛利率與 EPS 
略下修 
綜合上述，瀚荃將持續掌握 AI 伺服器電力架構升級商機。受惠伺
服器與網通業務明顯優於公司原先全年成長  10% YoY 的保守假
設，加上 4 月營收優於預期，研究部上修 2026 年營收預估，由 
40.09 億元上修至 40.65 億元（+21.1% YoY）。惟考量原物料價
格仍處高檔，且中國電鍍費用跳升將持續壓抑毛利率表現，因此將 
2026 年毛利率由 40.0% 下修至 38.7%（+1.3ppt YoY），EPS 由 
7.16 元下修至 6.78 元（+57.0% YoY）。展望 2027 年，隨 HVDC 
架構與 Sidecar 等新一代電力設計逐步導入，板端連接器用量與
規格可望持續提升，預估伺服器與網通業務仍可成長 24.3% YoY，
帶動 2027 年營收預估由 45.37 億元上修至 45.63 億元 （+12.2% 
YoY） 。 然而 ， 在原物料與電鍍成本壓力延續下 ， 研究部將 2027 年
毛利率由 41.1% 下修至 40.1%（+1.4ppt YoY），EPS 由 8.92 元
下修至 8.64 元（+27.4% YoY）。整體而言，在 AI Power 維持高
成長、伺服器與網通占比持續提升，加上新產能開出支撐下，瀚荃 
2026 至 2027 年營收與獲利續創新高。

7. 公開收購全宇昕 15% 股權，短期認列一次性費用，後續不排
除合作綜效 
瀚荃 1Q26 公開收購全宇昕 15% 股權，定位上以財務投資為主，
主要考量全與昕營運穩健、現金流表現良好，可作為公司中長期資
金配置的一環。短期財務影響方面，本次收購產生財務顧問費及相
關作業成本近 2,000 萬元，導致 1Q26 營業費用墊高；惟該費用
屬一次性支出，預期 2Q26 起費用將回到常態水準。後續來看，考
量雙方產品與客戶資源具一定互補性 ， 研究部不排除未來在功率元
件供應、區域通路拓展或交叉銷售上，出現更多業務合作與綜效。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言

司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。
